{\Huge\bfseries Algebraic Techniques for Rational Expressions\par}
\vspace{0.25in}
{\LARGE Factoring, Simplification, Polynomial Division, and Partial-Fraction Decomposition\par}
\vspace{0.45in}
{\Large A Recognition and Mastery Workbook\par}
\vfill
\begin{rulebox}{Purpose}
This is an algebra-only boot camp for students preparing to study calculus. It develops the ability to identify the shape of a polynomial or rational-expression problem, select the appropriate algebraic tool or sequence of tools, carry out the work correctly, preserve restrictions, and verify the result. No limits, derivatives, or integrals are used.
\end{rulebox}
\vfill
{\large Printable textbook edition\par}
\end{titlepage}
\hypersetup{pageanchor=true}
\cleardoublepage
\tableofcontents
\cleardoublepage
\chapter*{How to Use This Book}
\addcontentsline{toc}{chapter}{How to Use This Book}
This workbook is deliberately organized by tool before ending with an unlabeled capstone. While working inside a chapter, first learn the recognition cues and procedure. In the capstone, no chapter heading tells you what to do: you must diagnose the expression yourself.
\toolflow
Each technique chapter contains fifty problems in five levels. Problems 1--10 are direct. Problems 11--20 add routine variations. Problems 21--30 disguise the pattern or require preparation. Problems 31--40 combine stages. Problems 41--50 remove procedural hints.
\begin{rulebox}{Standing rules}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*]
\item Factor completely before cancelling or writing a partial-fraction template.
\item Cancel factors, never individual terms connected by addition or subtraction.
\item A rational expression is defined only where every original denominator is nonzero.
\item Cancelling a factor does not restore a value excluded by the original expression.
\item A partial-fraction decomposition begins only after the fraction is proper and the denominator is completely factored.
\item Verification is part of the procedure, not an optional extra.
\end{enumerate}
\end{rulebox}
\chapter*{Diagnostic Vocabulary}
\addcontentsline{toc}{chapter}{Diagnostic Vocabulary}
\begin{longtable}{>{\bfseries}p{0.22\textwidth}p{0.70\textwidth}}
Term & Meaning\\\toprule
Term & A piece separated from another piece by addition or subtraction.\\
Factor & A piece multiplied by another piece. Only factors can be cancelled.\\
Proper rational expression & $P(x)/Q(x)$ with $\deg P<\deg Q$.\\
Improper rational expression & $P(x)/Q(x)$ with $\deg P\ge\deg Q$. Polynomial division is required before partial fractions.\\
Distinct factor & A factor that occurs once.\\
Repeated factor & A factor occurring to a power greater than one.\\
Irreducible quadratic & A real quadratic with negative discriminant; it has no real linear factors.\\
Complete factorization & A factorization in which no allowed factoring tool applies to any remaining factor over the stated number system.\\
Restriction & A value excluded because an original denominator is zero or, in division, because the divisor is zero.\\
Identity & An equation true for every value for which both sides are defined.\\
\end{longtable}
\mainmatter

\part{Part I: Factoring Toolbox}
\chapter*{Factoring Strategy}
\addcontentsline{toc}{chapter}{Factoring Strategy}
Factoring reverses multiplication. Begin with the greatest common factor because it simplifies every later pattern. Then count terms: two terms suggest squares or cubes; three terms suggest a perfect square or quadratic trinomial; four or more terms may suggest grouping. Higher powers may hide a quadratic pattern.
\begin{rulebox}{Recommended order}
GCF $\longrightarrow$ special products $\longrightarrow$ trinomials $\longrightarrow$ grouping or substitution $\longrightarrow$ inspect every new factor again.
\end{rulebox}
\chapter{Greatest Common Factor and Sign Extraction}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Factor out the largest numerical and variable factor shared by every term.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
Look for a common coefficient, a lowest shared power of each variable, or a leading negative sign.
\section{Procedure}
First remove the GCF. Factoring out $-1$ is often useful when it makes the leading term positive.

\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Removing only part of the common factor, or forgetting that extracting a negative changes every sign inside the parentheses.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} $ 42 x^{3} + 66 x^{2} $

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is common numerical/monomial factor. The appropriate tool is: extract the greatest common factor.

\textbf{Result.} $ 6 x^{2} \left(7 x + 11\right) $

\textbf{Check.} Multiply the GCF by the remaining polynomial.
\end{examplebox}

\chapter{Difference of Squares}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Recognize and factor a subtraction of two perfect squares.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
Exactly two terms; subtraction; both terms are squares.
\section{Procedure}
Use $A^2-B^2=(A-B)(A+B)$, then inspect the new factors again.

\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Applying the formula to a sum of squares. Over the real numbers, $A^2+B^2$ does not factor by the difference-of-squares identity.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} $ x^{2} - 9 $

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is difference of two perfect squares. The appropriate tool is: apply $A^2-B^2=(A-B)(A+B)$ repeatedly.

\textbf{Result.} $ \left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right) $

\textbf{Check.} Expand the two conjugate factors.
\end{examplebox}

\chapter{Perfect-Square Trinomials}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Recognize a trinomial produced by squaring a binomial.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
The first and last terms are squares, and the middle term is $\pm2AB$.
\section{Procedure}
Use $A^2\pm2AB+B^2=(A\pm B)^2$.

\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Checking only the first and last terms. The middle term must be exactly $\pm2AB$.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} $ 16 x^{2} - 16 x + 4 $

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is three-term perfect-square pattern. The appropriate tool is: match $A^2\pm2AB+B^2$.

\textbf{Result.} $ 4 \left(2 x - 1\right)^{2} $

\textbf{Check.} Square the binomial.
\end{examplebox}

\chapter{Sum and Difference of Cubes}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Factor sums and differences of perfect cubes.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
Exactly two terms; each term is a cube; the sign may be plus or minus.
\section{Procedure}
Use $A^3-B^3=(A-B)(A^2+AB+B^2)$ and $A^3+B^3=(A+B)(A^2-AB+B^2)$.

\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Losing the sign pattern in the quadratic factor: the first sign matches the binomial and the middle sign is the opposite.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} $ 64 x^{3} + 8 $

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is sum or difference of cubes. The appropriate tool is: apply the cube identity, after any GCF.

\textbf{Result.} $ 8 \left(2 x + 1\right) \left(4 x^{2} - 2 x + 1\right) $

\textbf{Check.} Multiply the binomial and quadratic factors.
\end{examplebox}

\chapter{Monic Quadratic Trinomials}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Factor $x^2+bx+c$ over the integers when possible.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
A quadratic trinomial whose leading coefficient is $1$.
\section{Procedure}
Find integers $r,s$ with $r+s=b$ and $rs=c$; then $x^2+bx+c=(x+r)(x+s)$.

\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Choosing numbers with the correct product but the wrong sum.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} $ x^{2} - 6 x + 8 $

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is monic quadratic trinomial. The appropriate tool is: find two integers with sum $-b$ and product $c$.

\textbf{Result.} $ \left(x - 4\right) \left(x - 2\right) $

\textbf{Check.} Multiply the two binomials.
\end{examplebox}
\section{Mastery exercises}
\subsection*{Level I: direct recognition}
\begin{multicols}{2}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $ x^{2} - 9 x + 8 $
\item $ x^{2} - 9 x + 20 $
\item $ x^{2} - 6 x + 8 $
\item $ x^{2} - 8 x + 12 $
\item $ x^{2} - 9 x + 14 $
\item $ x^{2} - 3 x + 2 $

\chapter{General Quadratic Trinomials}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Factor $ax^2+bx+c$ when $a
e1$.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
A quadratic trinomial with a nonunit leading coefficient.
\section{Procedure}
Find a middle-term split whose product is $ac$ and whose sum is $b$, then factor by grouping.

\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Finding a middle-term split but failing to factor the resulting four terms by grouping.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} $ 6 x^{2} + 23 x + 21 $

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is nonmonic quadratic trinomial. The appropriate tool is: split the middle term or use an $ac$ method, then group.

\textbf{Result.} $ \left(2 x + 3\right) \left(3 x + 7\right) $

\textbf{Check.} Multiply the two binomials and compare all three coefficients.
\end{examplebox}

\chapter{Factoring by Grouping}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Create and extract a repeated binomial factor.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
Usually four terms that can be paired so each pair produces the same binomial.
\section{Procedure}
Group, factor each group, then factor out the common binomial.

\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Factoring each group correctly but producing opposite binomials; factor out $-1$ from one group when necessary.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} $ - 2 x^{3} + 12 x^{2} - 15 x - 4 $

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is four or more terms with a repeated binomial after grouping. The appropriate tool is: group terms and extract the common binomial.

\textbf{Result.} $ - \left(x - 4\right) \left(2 x^{2} - 4 x - 1\right) $

\textbf{Check.} Distribute the common binomial.
\end{examplebox}

\chapter{Substitution and Higher-Power Patterns}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Treat expressions such as $x^4-5x^2+4$ as quadratics in a power of $x$.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
Only powers $x^{2k},x^k,1$ occur for one fixed $k$.
\section{Procedure}
Set $u=x^k$, factor in $u$, replace $u$, and continue factoring.

\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Factoring in the temporary variable $u$ but forgetting to replace $u$ by the original power and continue factoring.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} $ x^{4} - 3 x^{2} + 2 $

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is quadratic pattern in $x^2$. The appropriate tool is: substitute $u=x^2$, factor in $u$, then replace $u$.

\textbf{Result.} $ \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right) \left(x^{2} - 2\right) $

\textbf{Check.} Expand after replacing the temporary variable.
\end{examplebox}

\chapter{Multi-Stage Complete Factorization}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Combine several factoring tools in the correct order.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
The first factorization reveals another recognizable shape.
\section{Procedure}
Use GCF first, then identities, trinomials, grouping, or substitution until no listed factorization applies.

\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Stopping after the first successful step instead of checking every new factor.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} $ 5 x^{5} + 15 x^{4} - 15 x^{3} + 15 x^{2} - 20 x $

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is expression containing more than one factoring pattern. The appropriate tool is: GCF first, then repeatedly factor until no listed tool applies.

\textbf{Result.} $ 5 x \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 4\right) \left(x^{2} + 1\right) $

\textbf{Check.} Multiply all final factors.
\end{examplebox}

\part{Part II: Rational-Expression Operations}
\chapter*{Rational-Expression Discipline}
\addcontentsline{toc}{chapter}{Rational-Expression Discipline}
A rational expression is a fraction built from polynomials. Its algebra is ordinary fraction algebra, but factoring controls cancellation and denominator construction. Record restrictions from the original expression before simplifying; those restrictions survive even when a factor later cancels.
\begin{rulebox}{Terms are not factors}
In $x+3$, neither $x$ nor $3$ is a factor of the entire expression. In $x(x+3)$, both $x$ and $x+3$ are factors. Cancellation applies only to a common nonzero factor of an entire numerator and denominator.
\end{rulebox}
\chapter{Simplification by Factoring and Cancellation}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Simplify a rational expression without losing domain restrictions.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
Numerator and denominator may share factors after factoring.
\section{Procedure}
Record original restrictions, factor completely, cancel identical nonzero factors only, and retain every original excluded value.

\begin{warningbox}{Illegal cancellation}
$\frac{x+3}{x}\ne3$ and $\frac{x^2+4}{x}\ne x+4$. Addition does not create a common factor. Factor first; cancel only an entire common factor.
\end{warningbox}
\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Cancelling terms rather than factors, or deleting an original excluded value after cancellation.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 7 x + 12}{x^{2} - 3 x - 4}$

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is quotient with common polynomial factors. The appropriate tool is: factor both parts, cancel factors only, preserve original restrictions.

\textbf{Result.} $\displaystyle \frac{x - 3}{x + 1}$, with $x\ne -1,\,4$.

\textbf{Check.} Multiply back or test an allowed value; recheck the restriction list.
\end{examplebox}

\chapter{Multiplication of Rational Expressions}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Multiply rational expressions efficiently.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
A product of fractions whose factors may cancel across numerators and denominators.
\section{Procedure}
Record restrictions, factor, cancel common factors across the entire product, then multiply what remains.

\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Multiplying expanded polynomials before looking for cancellable factors.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 1\right)}\cdot\frac{x \left(x + 1\right)}{\left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 4\right)}$

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is product of rational expressions. The appropriate tool is: factor, cancel across the product, then multiply.

\textbf{Result.} $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - x}{x^{2} - x - 20}$, with $x\ne -4,\,-3,\,-1,\,5$.

\textbf{Check.} Multiply the simplified factors and compare at an allowed value.
\end{examplebox}
\section{Mastery exercises}
\subsection*{Level I: direct recognition}
\begin{multicols}{2}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x - 8\right) \left(x + 2\right)}\cdot\frac{\left(x - 8\right) \left(x - 4\right)}{x \left(x - 2\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 8\right) \left(x + 4\right)}\cdot\frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 5\right) \left(x + 7\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 1\right)}\cdot\frac{x \left(x + 1\right)}{\left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 4\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x - 1\right)}\cdot\frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 2\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 2\right)}\cdot\frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x + 5\right) \left(x + 6\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 5\right)}\cdot\frac{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 6\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 8\right)}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 7\right)}\cdot\frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 6\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x \left(x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x - 2\right)}\cdot\frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x - 8\right) \left(x + 1\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x + 6\right)}{x \left(x + 1\right)}\cdot\frac{x \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x + 4\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x + 8\right)}{\left(x - 8\right) \left(x - 6\right)}\cdot\frac{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 8\right)}$
\end{enumerate}
\end{multicols}
\subsection*{Level II: routine variations}
\begin{multicols}{2}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 8\right) \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x - 2\right)}\cdot\frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 7\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 8\right)}\cdot\frac{\left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 6\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 7\right)}\cdot\frac{\left(x - 8\right) \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x - 1\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x + 5\right) \left(x + 8\right)}{x \left(x - 4\right)}\cdot\frac{x \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 8\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 8\right) \left(x - 4\right)}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)}\cdot\frac{\left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 8\right) \left(x - 6\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x - 1\right)}\cdot\frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 5\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x + 4\right)}\cdot\frac{x \left(x - 7\right)}{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 8\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x + 6\right) \left(x + 8\right)}{\left(x - 8\right) \left(x - 7\right)}\cdot\frac{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 6\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 8\right)}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 5\right)}\cdot\frac{\left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 5\right)}{x \left(x + 8\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x + 8\right)}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x - 1\right)}\cdot\frac{x \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x - 6\right)}$
\end{enumerate}
\end{multicols}
\subsection*{Level III: disguised or prepared forms}
\begin{multicols}{2}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 4 x - 5}{x^{2} - 2 x - 8}\cdot\frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 8\right)}{\left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 5\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 11 x + 30}{x^{2} - x}\cdot\frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 8\right)}{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 6\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}{x^{2} + 5 x}\cdot\frac{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 4\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 18}{x^{2} + x - 2}\cdot\frac{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 8\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 6 x}{x^{2} - 11 x + 24}\cdot\frac{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x - 1\right)}{x \left(x + 1\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 15 x + 56}{x^{2} + x - 12}\cdot\frac{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x - 8\right) \left(x - 6\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 3 x - 10}{x^{2} + 12 x + 32}\cdot\frac{\left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 8\right)}{\left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 5\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 4}{x^{2} - 4 x}\cdot\frac{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x - 4\right)}{\left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 5\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 12 x + 35}{x^{2} + 5 x + 4}\cdot\frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 1\right)}{x \left(x + 7\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 2 x - 24}{x^{2} + 6 x - 7}\cdot\frac{\left(x + 7\right) \left(x + 8\right)}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x - 3\right)}$
\end{enumerate}
\end{multicols}
\subsection*{Level IV: multi-stage execution}
\begin{multicols}{2}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 10}{x^{2} + 3 x - 40}\cdot\frac{x^{2} + x - 30}{x^{2} - x - 30}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 11 x + 24}{x^{2} - x - 6}\cdot\frac{x^{2} + 4 x - 21}{x^{2} - 2 x - 15}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 10 x + 24}{x^{2} - 4 x - 32}\cdot\frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}{x^{2} - 13 x + 42}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 8 x + 15}{x^{2} + x - 56}\cdot\frac{x^{2} - 11 x + 28}{x^{2} - 11 x + 24}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 2 x - 24}{x^{2} + 11 x + 24}\cdot\frac{x^{2} - 2 x - 15}{x^{2} + 13 x + 42}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 2 x - 3}{x^{2} + 12 x + 32}\cdot\frac{x^{2} - 3 x - 28}{x^{2} + 7 x + 6}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 6 x + 8}{x^{2} + 15 x + 56}\cdot\frac{x^{2} + 11 x + 24}{x^{2} + 3 x + 2}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 10 x + 16}{x^{2} + x - 42}\cdot\frac{x^{2} + 7 x}{x^{2} + 5 x - 24}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 8 x}{x^{2} + 6 x - 7}\cdot\frac{x^{2} + x - 2}{x^{2} - 64}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 11 x + 24}{x^{2} + x - 20}\cdot\frac{x^{2} - 25}{x^{2} + 15 x + 56}$
\end{enumerate}
\end{multicols}
\subsection*{Level V: independent mastery}
\begin{multicols}{2}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 2 x - 3}{x^{2} - 4}\cdot\frac{x^{2} - 4 x - 12}{x^{2} - 1}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 6 x + 8}{x^{2} - 64}\cdot\frac{x^{2} + x - 56}{x^{2} - 2 x - 24}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - x - 42}{x^{2} - 11 x + 30}\cdot\frac{x^{2} - 6 x + 5}{x^{2} - 6 x - 7}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 8}{x^{2} - 8 x}\cdot\frac{x^{2} + 3 x}{x^{2} + 6 x - 16}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + x - 2}{x^{2} - 2 x - 24}\cdot\frac{x^{2} - 6 x}{x^{2} + 7 x - 8}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 3 x + 2}{x^{2} + 10 x + 24}\cdot\frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 20}{x^{2} + 2 x}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 5 x + 6}{x^{2} - 5 x}\cdot\frac{x^{2} + 3 x - 40}{x^{2} + 8 x + 15}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + x - 2}{x^{2} + 4 x - 32}\cdot\frac{x^{2} + 3 x - 28}{x^{2} - 4 x + 3}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 10 x + 21}{x^{2} - 64}\cdot\frac{x^{2} - 12 x + 32}{x^{2} - 7 x}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 7 x - 8}{x^{2} - 7 x - 8}\cdot\frac{x^{2} + 3 x + 2}{x^{2} - 4 x + 3}$
\end{enumerate}
\end{multicols}
\chapter{Division of Rational Expressions}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Divide rational expressions correctly.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
A quotient in which the divisor is itself a rational expression.
\section{Procedure}
Require all original denominators and the divisor numerator to be nonzero, multiply by the reciprocal, factor, and simplify.

\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Forgetting that the divisor itself must be nonzero, so its numerator creates additional restrictions.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 2\right)}\div\frac{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x - 1\right)}$

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is quotient of rational expressions. The appropriate tool is: record restrictions, multiply by the reciprocal, factor, and cancel.

\textbf{Result.} $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 9 x + 14}{x^{2} + 9 x + 14}$, with $x\ne -7,\,-2,\,1,\,6,\,7$.

\textbf{Check.} Multiply by the original divisor and compare at an allowed value.
\end{examplebox}
\section{Mastery exercises}
\subsection*{Level I: direct recognition}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 5\right)}{x \left(x + 8\right)}\div\frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x + 8\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 6\right) \left(x + 8\right)}\div\frac{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 6\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 2\right)}\div\frac{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x - 1\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 1\right)}\div\frac{x \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 8\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x + 6\right) \left(x + 8\right)}\div\frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 7\right)}{\left(x - 8\right) \left(x + 8\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 3\right)}\div\frac{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 1\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 8\right)}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 6\right)}\div\frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 5\right)}{x \left(x - 2\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 8\right) \left(x - 3\right)}\div\frac{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x - 2\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x - 4\right)}\div\frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x - 3\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 2\right)}\div\frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 5\right)}$
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{Level II: routine variations}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 6\right)}{x \left(x + 2\right)}\div\frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 2\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x - 1\right)}\div\frac{\left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x - 1\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 7\right)}\div\frac{\left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 8\right)}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 7\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 8\right) \left(x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x - 3\right)}\div\frac{\left(x - 8\right) \left(x - 1\right)}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 8\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 2\right)}\div\frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 2\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 8\right) \left(x - 7\right)}\div\frac{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x + 5\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x - 6\right)}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 6\right)}\div\frac{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 1\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 7\right)}\div\frac{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 7\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 1\right)}{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x + 7\right)}\div\frac{\left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 3\right)}{x \left(x - 7\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 4\right)}\div\frac{\left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x + 3\right)}$
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{Level III: disguised or prepared forms}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 8 x + 15}{x^{2} + 6 x - 7}\div\frac{x \left(x + 5\right)}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 8\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 4 x - 12}{x^{2} - 8 x}\div\frac{\left(x + 4\right) \left(x + 6\right)}{\left(x - 8\right) \left(x - 3\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 7 x - 8}{x^{2} - 8 x}\div\frac{\left(x + 4\right) \left(x + 8\right)}{x \left(x - 2\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + x - 6}{x^{2} - x - 30}\div\frac{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 5\right) \left(x + 7\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 3 x - 4}{x^{2} + x - 12}\div\frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 3\right)}{\left(x + 4\right) \left(x + 7\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 3 x - 10}{x^{2} - x - 20}\div\frac{x \left(x - 2\right)}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 1\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 4 x - 32}{x^{2} + 2 x - 24}\div\frac{x \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x - 4\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 9 x + 18}{x^{2} - 2 x - 48}\div\frac{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 4\right)}{\left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 6\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 12 x + 32}{x^{2} + 2 x}\div\frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 5\right)}{x \left(x + 7\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 16}{x^{2} + 10 x + 21}\div\frac{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 2\right)}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 7\right)}$
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{Level IV: multi-stage execution}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 8 x}{x^{2} - 2 x - 3}\div\frac{x^{2} + 6 x - 16}{x^{2} - 7 x - 8}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 6 x + 5}{x^{2} + 9 x + 18}\div\frac{x^{2} - 25}{x^{2} - 9}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 4}{x^{2} - 10 x + 24}\div\frac{x^{2} + 6 x + 8}{x^{2} - 5 x - 6}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 11 x + 30}{x^{2} - x - 6}\div\frac{x^{2} + 5 x}{x^{2} - 2 x - 3}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 7 x + 6}{x^{2} - 8 x}\div\frac{x^{2} + x - 2}{x^{2} - 64}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 7 x - 8}{x^{2} + 3 x - 28}\div\frac{x^{2} - 4 x + 3}{x^{2} + 9 x + 14}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 4 x - 32}{x^{2} - 4}\div\frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 12}{x^{2} + 3 x + 2}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 2 x - 35}{x^{2} - 9 x + 18}\div\frac{x^{2} - 4 x - 21}{x^{2} + 4 x - 21}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - x - 6}{x^{2} - 10 x + 16}\div\frac{x^{2} + 5 x + 6}{x^{2} - 3 x - 40}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 18}{x^{2} - 2 x - 3}\div\frac{x^{2} - 36}{x^{2} - 1}$
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{Level V: independent mastery}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 4 x - 21}{x^{2} - 7 x}\div\frac{x^{2} + 13 x + 42}{x^{2} - 6 x}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 11 x + 24}{x^{2} + 2 x}\div\frac{x^{2} - 7 x - 8}{x^{2} - 5 x}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 3 x - 18}{x^{2} + 2 x - 8}\div\frac{x^{2} + 4 x - 21}{x^{2} - 16}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 9 x + 8}{x^{2} - 7 x + 12}\div\frac{x^{2} + x - 2}{x^{2} + 2 x - 24}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 3 x - 4}{x^{2} - 12 x + 35}\div\frac{x^{2} + 6 x + 8}{x^{2} - 13 x + 42}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 7 x + 6}{x^{2} + 3 x - 28}\div\frac{x^{2} - 2 x - 48}{x^{2} - 2 x - 8}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 3 x - 18}{x^{2} + 5 x - 6}\div\frac{x^{2} - 2 x - 15}{x^{2} + 14 x + 48}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 4 x - 21}{x^{2} - 11 x + 24}\div\frac{x^{2} - 3 x - 18}{x^{2} - 7 x - 8}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 1}{x^{2} - 36}\div\frac{x^{2} - 3 x + 2}{x^{2} + x - 30}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + x - 56}{x^{2} + 6 x - 7}\div\frac{x^{2} - 4 x - 21}{x^{2} + x - 42}$
\end{enumerate}
\chapter{Addition and Subtraction of Rational Expressions}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Build a least common denominator and combine rational expressions.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
A sum or difference of fractions, often with related denominators.
\section{Procedure}
Factor denominators, choose the LCD using every factor at its greatest required power, rewrite, combine numerators, and simplify.

\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Using a common denominator that omits a repeated factor or uses too small a power.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} $\displaystyle \frac{-2}{x - 8}+\frac{-1}{x}$

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is sum or difference with unlike denominators. The appropriate tool is: factor denominators, build the LCD, combine, and simplify.

\textbf{Result.} $\displaystyle \frac{- 3 x + 8}{x^{2} - 8 x}$, with $x\ne 0,\,8$.

\textbf{Check.} Put the answer over the LCD and compare numerators.
\end{examplebox}
\section{Mastery exercises}
\subsection*{Level I: direct recognition}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-2}{x + 5}-\frac{1}{x - 4}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{6}{x - 4}+\frac{3}{x + 4}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-2}{x - 8}+\frac{-1}{x}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{5}{x + 3}+\frac{-2}{x - 8}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-2}{x + 6}+\frac{-1}{x - 7}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{6}{x + 5}+\frac{-1}{x + 1}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-6}{x - 3}-\frac{4}{x - 6}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-3}{x - 5}+\frac{-2}{x - 3}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-6}{x + 8}+\frac{3}{x - 5}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{4}{x - 8}-\frac{4}{x + 8}$
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{Level II: routine variations}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle \frac{2}{x - 3}-\frac{-2}{x - 5}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{5}{x}-\frac{-3}{x + 7}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{1}{x - 5}-\frac{-5}{x + 8}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-1}{x + 1}+\frac{-6}{x - 1}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-6}{x - 6}-\frac{1}{x + 7}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{2}{x - 3}-\frac{2}{x + 1}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{6}{x - 6}+\frac{-4}{x - 8}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{3}{x - 4}-\frac{1}{x - 2}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{1}{x - 4}-\frac{-4}{x - 1}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-5}{x + 7}+\frac{3}{x - 8}$
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{Level III: disguised or prepared forms}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-3}{\left(x + 7\right)^{2}}-\frac{-4}{x \left(x + 7\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{5}{\left(x + 8\right)^{2}}+\frac{6}{\left(x + 6\right) \left(x + 8\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-4}{\left(x - 6\right)^{2}}-\frac{-5}{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 8\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{3}{\left(x + 6\right)^{2}}+\frac{-4}{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 6\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{2}{\left(x + 2\right)^{2}}-\frac{3}{\left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 3\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-6}{\left(x - 8\right)^{2}}+\frac{-3}{\left(x - 8\right) \left(x + 3\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{5}{\left(x + 2\right)^{2}}+\frac{3}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 2\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-6}{\left(x + 7\right)^{2}}-\frac{6}{\left(x + 4\right) \left(x + 7\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-3}{\left(x + 4\right)^{2}}+\frac{-6}{\left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 4\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-3}{\left(x - 1\right)^{2}}-\frac{4}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x - 1\right)}$
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{Level IV: multi-stage execution}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle \frac{3}{\left(x - 2\right)^{2}}+\frac{2}{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x - 2\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{4}{\left(x + 3\right)^{2}}+\frac{3}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 3\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{5}{\left(x + 6\right)^{2}}-\frac{-6}{\left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 6\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-1}{\left(x - 3\right)^{2}}+\frac{3}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 7\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{3}{\left(x - 8\right)^{2}}+\frac{6}{\left(x - 8\right) \left(x - 7\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-2}{\left(x + 4\right)^{2}}-\frac{4}{x \left(x + 4\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{3}{\left(x + 5\right)^{2}}-\frac{2}{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 5\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-6}{\left(x - 6\right)^{2}}+\frac{4}{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x - 4\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-1}{\left(x + 2\right)^{2}}-\frac{-4}{\left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 5\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{1}{\left(x + 8\right)^{2}}+\frac{2}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 8\right)}$
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{Level V: independent mastery}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-2}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x^{2} + 1\right)}-\frac{-1}{\left(x + 2\right) \left(x^{2} + 1\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-1}{\left(x + 4\right) \left(x^{2} + 1\right)}-\frac{4}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x^{2} + 1\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-5}{\left(x + 5\right) \left(x^{2} + 1\right)}+\frac{-3}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x^{2} + 1\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{6}{\left(x - 8\right) \left(x^{2} + 1\right)}+\frac{4}{\left(x + 4\right) \left(x^{2} + 1\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-4}{\left(x + 8\right) \left(x^{2} + 1\right)}-\frac{1}{\left(x + 6\right) \left(x^{2} + 1\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{4}{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x^{2} + 1\right)}-\frac{-6}{x \left(x^{2} + 1\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-2}{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x^{2} + 1\right)}-\frac{2}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x^{2} + 1\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{1}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x^{2} + 1\right)}+\frac{-5}{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x^{2} + 1\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-5}{\left(x + 1\right) \left(x^{2} + 1\right)}+\frac{-1}{\left(x + 6\right) \left(x^{2} + 1\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{2}{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x^{2} + 1\right)}+\frac{2}{\left(x + 3\right) \left(x^{2} + 1\right)}$
\end{enumerate}
\chapter{Complex Rational Expressions}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Simplify a large fraction containing smaller fractions.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
Fractions appear in the numerator, denominator, or both.
\section{Procedure}
Find the LCD of all small denominators and multiply every term in the large numerator and denominator by it.

\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Multiplying only some terms by the internal LCD. Every term in both the large numerator and large denominator must be multiplied.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{x}{x - 2}}{\frac{x + 6}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 5\right)}}$

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is fraction whose numerator or denominator contains fractions. The appropriate tool is: identify the internal LCD and clear all small denominators.

\textbf{Result.} $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 5 x}{x + 6}$, with $x\ne -6,\,-5,\,2$.

\textbf{Check.} Reverse the clearing step or test an allowed value.
\end{examplebox}
\section{Mastery exercises}
\subsection*{Level I: direct recognition}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{1}{x + 2} + \frac{1}{x - 6}}{\frac{1}{x + 2} - \frac{1}{x - 6}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{3}{x + 6} + \frac{3}{x - 5}}{\frac{1}{x + 6}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{x}{x - 2}}{\frac{x + 6}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 5\right)}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{5}{x^{2} - 1} + \frac{1}{x + 2}}{\frac{1}{x + 2}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{1}{x + 1} + \frac{1}{x - 5}}{- \frac{1}{x + 1} + \frac{1}{x - 5}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{- \frac{5}{x + 3} - \frac{1}{x + 1}}{\frac{1}{x + 3}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{x}{x + 6}}{\frac{x + 4}{\left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 6\right)}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{5}{x^{2} - 1} + \frac{1}{x + 5}}{\frac{1}{x + 5}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{1}{x + 4} + \frac{1}{x - 5}}{- \frac{1}{x + 4} + \frac{1}{x - 5}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{2}{x - 1} + \frac{1}{x - 3}}{\frac{1}{x - 3}}$
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{Level II: routine variations}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{x}{x + 3}}{\frac{x + 4}{\left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 5\right)}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{- \frac{5}{x^{2} - 1} + \frac{1}{x + 3}}{\frac{1}{x + 3}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{1}{x + 4} + \frac{1}{x - 5}}{- \frac{1}{x + 4} + \frac{1}{x - 5}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{2}{x - 3} - \frac{2}{x}}{\frac{1}{x}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{x}{x + 3}}{\frac{x - 2}{\left(x - 3\right) \left(x + 3\right)}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{2}{x^{2} - 1} + \frac{1}{x}}{\frac{1}{x}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{1}{x + 3} + \frac{1}{x - 5}}{- \frac{1}{x + 3} + \frac{1}{x - 5}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{2}{x + 4} + \frac{4}{x}}{\frac{1}{x + 4}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{1}{\frac{x - 4}{x \left(x - 5\right)}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{4}{x^{2} - 1} + \frac{1}{x + 1}}{\frac{1}{x + 1}}$
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{Level III: disguised or prepared forms}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{1}{x + 6} + \frac{1}{x - 2}}{\frac{1}{x + 6} - \frac{1}{x - 2}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{- \frac{1}{x - 2} + \frac{2}{x - 4}}{\frac{1}{x - 2}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{x}{x + 2}}{\frac{x + 3}{\left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 4\right)}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{- \frac{2}{x^{2} - 1} + \frac{1}{x + 1}}{\frac{1}{x + 1}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{1}{x + 5} + \frac{1}{x - 3}}{- \frac{1}{x + 5} + \frac{1}{x - 3}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{- \frac{5}{x - 3} + \frac{5}{x - 4}}{\frac{1}{x - 3}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{x}{x + 2}}{\frac{x + 5}{\left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 4\right)}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{- \frac{3}{x^{2} - 1} + \frac{1}{x}}{\frac{1}{x}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{1}{x + 6} + \frac{1}{x - 4}}{- \frac{1}{x + 6} + \frac{1}{x - 4}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{- \frac{4}{x + 6} + \frac{2}{x - 3}}{\frac{1}{x - 3}}$
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{Level IV: multi-stage execution}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{x}{x + 3}}{\frac{x + 4}{\left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 5\right)}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{- \frac{3}{x^{2} - 1} + \frac{1}{x - 3}}{\frac{1}{x - 3}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{1}{x + 2} + \frac{1}{x + 1}}{\frac{1}{x + 2} - \frac{1}{x + 1}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{1}{x - 2} + \frac{2}{x}}{\frac{1}{x - 2}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{x}{x + 2}}{\frac{x - 6}{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x + 2\right)}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{- \frac{2}{x^{2} - 1} + \frac{1}{x - 3}}{\frac{1}{x - 3}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{1}{x + 2} + \frac{1}{x + 1}}{- \frac{1}{x + 2} + \frac{1}{x + 1}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{- \frac{5}{x + 6} - \frac{3}{x}}{\frac{1}{x}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{x}{x - 5}}{\frac{x - 2}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x - 3\right)}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{- \frac{5}{x^{2} - 1} + \frac{1}{x}}{\frac{1}{x}}$
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{Level V: independent mastery}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{1}{x + 2} + \frac{1}{x - 6}}{\frac{1}{x + 2} - \frac{1}{x - 6}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{- \frac{5}{x - 6} + \frac{1}{x}}{\frac{1}{x - 6}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{x}{x - 2}}{\frac{x}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x - 1\right)}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{2}{x^{2} - 1} + \frac{1}{x - 4}}{\frac{1}{x - 4}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{1}{x + 1} + \frac{1}{x - 2}}{- \frac{1}{x + 1} + \frac{1}{x - 2}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{5}{x + 5} - \frac{4}{x + 1}}{\frac{1}{x + 5}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{x}{x - 2}}{\frac{x - 5}{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x - 2\right)}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{5}{x^{2} - 1} + \frac{1}{x + 1}}{\frac{1}{x + 1}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{1}{x + 6} + \frac{1}{x - 3}}{\frac{1}{x + 6} - \frac{1}{x - 3}}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{\frac{1}{x + 6} + \frac{2}{x}}{\frac{1}{x}}$
\end{enumerate}
\part{Part III: Polynomial Division}
\chapter*{Polynomial Division as an Algorithm}
\addcontentsline{toc}{chapter}{Polynomial Division as an Algorithm}
Polynomial division is organized bookkeeping. At each stage, choose the next quotient term so that multiplication by the divisor cancels the current leading term. The invariant is
\[
P(x)=D(x)Q(x)+R(x),\qquad \deg R<\deg D.
\]
Synthetic division records the same arithmetic in compressed form when the divisor is $x-c$.

\chapter{Polynomial Long Division}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Find quotient and remainder for arbitrary polynomial divisors.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
The divisor is a polynomial and the dividend degree is at least as large.
\section{Procedure}
Arrange descending powers, insert zero placeholders, divide leading terms, multiply, subtract, and continue until $\deg R<\deg D$.

\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Subtracting only the leading term of a product rather than the entire polynomial row.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} $\displaystyle (6 x^{3} - 5 x^{2} - 8 x + 4)\div(- 2 x + 3)$

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is polynomial dividend and nonzero polynomial divisor. The appropriate tool is: polynomial long division with zero placeholders as needed.

\textbf{Result.} $Q(x)=- 3 x^{2} - 2 x + 1$, $R(x)=1$.

\textbf{Check.} Verify $P=DQ+R$ and $\deg R<\deg D$.
\end{examplebox}
\section{Mastery exercises}
\subsection*{Level I: direct recognition}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle (- 4 x^{2} - 15 x + 4)\div(- x - 4)$
\item $\displaystyle (8 x^{3} - 14 x^{2} - 8 x)\div(- 4 x - 1)$
\item $\displaystyle (6 x^{3} - 5 x^{2} - 8 x + 4)\div(- 2 x + 3)$
\item $\displaystyle (9 x^{3} - 4 x + 5)\div(- 3 x + 1)$
\item $\displaystyle (- 12 x^{3} - 4 x^{2} + 4 x - 3)\div(4 x)$
\item $\displaystyle (- 8 x^{3} + 18 x^{2} - 8 x + 1)\div(- 4 x + 1)$
\item $\displaystyle (x^{2} + x + 1)\div(- x)$
\item $\displaystyle (- 3 x^{3} + 2 x^{2} + 2)\div(3 x - 2)$
\item $\displaystyle (- 6 x^{3} + 16 x^{2} - 17 x + 10)\div(- 3 x + 2)$
\item $\displaystyle (- 16 x^{2} + 32 x - 16)\div(4 x - 4)$
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{Level II: routine variations}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle (12 x^{3} - 5 x^{2} - 15 x + 9)\div(- 4 x + 3)$
\item $\displaystyle (- 3 x^{4} + 12 x^{3} - 6 x^{2} - 10 x + 5)\div(- x + 3)$
\item $\displaystyle (12 x^{3} - 25 x^{2} + 12)\div(- 4 x + 3)$
\item $\displaystyle (8 x^{2} - 16 x - 5)\div(- 4 x)$
\item $\displaystyle (- 4 x^{4} - 7 x^{3} + x^{2} + x - 4)\div(x + 1)$
\item $\displaystyle (4 x^{4} + 14 x^{3} - 24 x^{2} + 4 x + 2)\div(4 x - 2)$

\chapter{Synthetic Division}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Use the compressed division algorithm for divisors $x-c$.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
The divisor is exactly monic linear: $x-c$.
\section{Procedure}
Use $c$, list every coefficient including zeros, bring down, multiply, add, and interpret the last number as the remainder.

\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Using the visible constant instead of $c$: the divisor $x+4$ means $c=-4$.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} Divide $ - 2 x^{4} + 2 x^{3} - x^{2} + 13 x - 2 $ by $x - 2$.

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is linear divisor of the form $x-c$. The appropriate tool is: synthetic division using $c$ and every coefficient, including zeros.

\textbf{Result.} $Q(x)=- 2 x^{3} - 2 x^{2} - 5 x + 3$, $R=4$.

\textbf{Check.} Verify $P=(x-c)Q+R$.
\end{examplebox}

\chapter{Choosing Long or Synthetic Division}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Select the correct polynomial division method.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
Inspect the divisor before calculating.
\section{Procedure}
Ordinary synthetic division applies to $x-c$; polynomial long division always applies to a nonzero polynomial divisor.

\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Trying ordinary synthetic division on a divisor that is not exactly of the form $x-c$.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} Choose the ordinary method and divide $(3 x^{4} - 11 x^{3} + 14 x^{2} - 7 x + 19)$ by $(x^{2} - 4 x + 5)$.

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is inspect the divisor. The appropriate tool is: use synthetic division only for $x-c$; otherwise use long division.

\textbf{Result.} polynomial long division; $Q(x)=3 x^{2} + x + 3$, $R(x)=4$.

\textbf{Check.} Verify $P=DQ+R$.
\end{examplebox}

\part{Part IV: Partial-Fraction Decomposition}
\chapter*{Partial Fractions: The Master Setup}
\addcontentsline{toc}{chapter}{Partial Fractions: The Master Setup}
Partial-fraction decomposition reverses the addition of rational expressions. The denominator shape determines the template; the numerator determines the coefficients.
\begin{rulebox}{Prerequisite checklist}
Before solving for any coefficient: (1) divide if the fraction is improper; (2) factor the denominator completely; (3) classify every factor and multiplicity; (4) write the complete template.
\end{rulebox}
\begin{examplebox}{Distinct linear factors and cover-up}
For
\[
\frac{5x+1}{(x-1)(x+2)}=\frac{A}{x-1}+\frac{B}{x+2},
\]
multiply by the denominator:
\[
5x+1=A(x+2)+B(x-1).
\]
Set $x=1$ to obtain $6=3A$, so $A=2$. Set $x=-2$ to obtain $-9=-3B$, so $B=3$. Therefore
\[
\frac{5x+1}{(x-1)(x+2)}=\frac{2}{x-1}+\frac{3}{x+2}.
\]
Recombining the right side verifies the result.
\end{examplebox}
\begin{examplebox}{Repeated linear factor}
A denominator $(x-1)^2(x+2)$ requires
\[
\frac{A}{x-1}+\frac{B}{(x-1)^2}+\frac{C}{x+2}.
\]
Both powers of the repeated factor must appear. Root substitution isolates $B$ and $C$; a convenient extra value or coefficient comparison determines $A$.
\end{examplebox}
\begin{examplebox}{Irreducible quadratic factor}
A denominator $(x-1)(x^2+4)$ requires
\[
\frac{A}{x-1}+\frac{Bx+C}{x^2+4}.
\]
The numerator over the quadratic is linear because its degree must be strictly smaller than the quadratic degree.
\end{examplebox}
\chapter{Distinct Linear Factors}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Write and compute a partial-fraction decomposition with distinct linear factors.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
A proper rational expression whose denominator factors as distinct $x-r$.
\section{Procedure}
Place one constant numerator over each linear factor, clear denominators, and determine the constants.

\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Writing a template before making the fraction proper and factoring the denominator completely.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} $\displaystyle \frac{- 2 x - 46}{\left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 8\right)}$

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is proper rational expression with distinct linear denominator factors. The appropriate tool is: write the distinct-linear template and determine its constants.

\textbf{Result.} $\displaystyle \frac{5}{x + 8} - \frac{7}{x + 2}$.

\textbf{Check.} Recombine the simple fractions.
\end{examplebox}
\section{Mastery exercises}
\subsection*{Level I: direct recognition}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle \frac{6 x + 54}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 7\right)}$

\item $\displaystyle \frac{- x - 62}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 8\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{- 2 x + 18}{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x - 5\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{8 x + 14}{x \left(x + 7\right)}$
\item $\displaystyle \frac{-84}{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 8\right)}$
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{Level II: routine variations}

\chapter{Heaviside Cover-Up Method}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Rapidly isolate coefficients attached to distinct linear factors.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
A proper rational expression with distinct linear factors and no repeated power for the coefficient being covered.
\section{Procedure}
For the coefficient over $x-r$, multiply by $x-r$ and substitute $x=r$; then verify the complete decomposition.

\begin{warningbox}{Limit of ordinary cover-up}
Cover-up immediately isolates coefficients attached to distinct linear factors. Repeated factors and irreducible quadratics require additional substitution, coefficient comparison, or an advanced derivative formula. This workbook uses the elementary methods first.
\end{warningbox}
\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Using ordinary cover-up as though it determined every coefficient of a repeated factor.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} $\displaystyle \frac{8 x + 37}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 8\right)}$

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is proper rational expression with distinct linear denominator factors. The appropriate tool is: use the Heaviside cover-up method, then verify.

\textbf{Result.} $\displaystyle \frac{3}{x + 8} + \frac{5}{x - 1}$.

\textbf{Check.} Recombine after using cover-up.
\end{examplebox}

\chapter{Strategic Substitution}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Choose values that eliminate many unknown terms.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
After clearing denominators, special values make factors zero or simplify the identity.
\section{Procedure}
Use roots first, then convenient values such as $0,1,-1$ when they reduce the remaining system.

\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Substituting into the original fractional equation before first clearing denominators and forming a polynomial identity.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} Determine the constants by strategic substitution: $\displaystyle \frac{11 x + 20}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 5\right)}=\frac{A}{x + 5} + \frac{B}{x - 2}$.

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is coefficient identity with values that make terms vanish. The appropriate tool is: clear denominators, then choose roots and other convenient values.

\textbf{Result.} $\displaystyle \frac{5}{x + 5} + \frac{6}{x - 2}$.

\textbf{Check.} Substitute the coefficients into the cleared identity.
\end{examplebox}

\item Determine the constants by strategic substitution: $\displaystyle \frac{10 x^{2} - 111 x + 293}{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x - 5\right) \left(x - 4\right)}=\frac{A}{x - 4} + \frac{B}{x - 7} + \frac{C}{x - 5}$.
\item Determine the constants by strategic substitution: $\displaystyle \frac{- x^{2} + 26 x - 97}{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x - 4\right) \left(x - 3\right)}=\frac{A}{x - 3} + \frac{B}{x - 4} + \frac{C}{x - 7}$.
\item Determine the constants by strategic substitution: $\displaystyle \frac{11 x^{2} - 65 x - 4}{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 1\right)}=\frac{A}{x - 7} + \frac{B}{x - 2} + \frac{C}{x + 1}$.
\item Determine the constants by strategic substitution: $\displaystyle \frac{- 69 x + 366}{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 6\right)}=\frac{A}{x + 6} + \frac{B}{x - 7} + \frac{C}{x - 4}$.
\item Determine the constants by strategic substitution: $\displaystyle \frac{- 24 x - 156}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 5\right)}=\frac{A}{x + 5} + \frac{B}{x + 3} + \frac{C}{x - 4}$.
\end{enumerate}
\chapter{Equating Coefficients}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Determine constants from a polynomial identity.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
Clearing denominators produces polynomials equal for every allowed $x$.
\section{Procedure}
Expand, collect like powers, equate matching coefficients, and solve the resulting linear system.

\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Equating unlike powers or overlooking a missing power whose coefficient is zero.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} Use coefficient comparison to decompose $\displaystyle \frac{- 6 x + 12}{x \left(x - 3\right)}$.

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is polynomial identity after clearing denominators. The appropriate tool is: expand both sides and equate coefficients of equal powers.

\textbf{Result.} $\displaystyle - \frac{2}{x - 3} - \frac{4}{x}$.

\textbf{Check.} Compare the expanded coefficients on both sides.
\end{examplebox}
\section{Mastery exercises}
\subsection*{Level I: direct recognition}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item Use coefficient comparison to decompose $\displaystyle \frac{7 x + 4}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 4\right)}$.
\item Use coefficient comparison to decompose $\displaystyle \frac{-6}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 1\right)}$.

\item Use coefficient comparison to decompose $\displaystyle \frac{- 2 x + 18}{x \left(x - 6\right)}$.

\item Use coefficient comparison to decompose $\displaystyle \frac{- 8 x + 27}{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 1\right)}$.

\chapter{Repeated Linear Factors}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Build the complete template for repeated linear factors.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
The denominator contains $(x-r)^m$ with $m>1$.
\section{Procedure}
Include $A_1/(x-r)+A_2/(x-r)^2+\cdots+A_m/(x-r)^m$.

\begin{warningbox}{Template completeness}
Do not skip intermediate powers. A repeated factor contributes a separate numerator over every power from $1$ through its multiplicity.
\end{warningbox}
\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Omitting one of the powers $1,2,\ldots,m$ of a repeated linear factor.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} $\displaystyle \frac{- 5 x - 10}{\left(x + 1\right)^{2}}$

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is one or more repeated linear factors. The appropriate tool is: include a term for every power from $1$ through the multiplicity.

\textbf{Result.} $\displaystyle - \frac{5}{x + 1} - \frac{5}{\left(x + 1\right)^{2}}$.

\textbf{Check.} Recombine over the repeated denominator.
\end{examplebox}

\chapter{Distinct Irreducible Quadratic Factors}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Decompose over irreducible quadratic denominator factors.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
A quadratic factor has negative discriminant and does not factor over the reals.
\section{Procedure}
Use a general linear numerator $Ax+B$ over each irreducible quadratic.

\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Putting only a constant over an irreducible quadratic; the numerator must be a general linear polynomial.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} $\displaystyle \frac{- 4 x^{3} - 9 x^{2} - 48 x + 14}{\left(x^{2} - 2 x + 6\right) \left(x^{2} + 2 x + 8\right)}$

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is distinct irreducible quadratic factors. The appropriate tool is: place a general linear numerator over each quadratic factor.

\textbf{Result.} $\displaystyle \frac{- 5 x - 2}{x^{2} - 2 x + 6} + \frac{x + 5}{x^{2} + 2 x + 8}$.

\textbf{Check.} Recombine over the product of quadratics.
\end{examplebox}

\chapter{Repeated Irreducible Quadratic Factors}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Build the complete template for repeated quadratic factors.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
The denominator contains $q(x)^m$ where $q$ is irreducible quadratic.
\section{Procedure}
Include a separate linear numerator over every power $q,q^2,\ldots,q^m$.

\begin{warningbox}{Template completeness}
Do not skip intermediate powers. A repeated factor contributes a separate numerator over every power from $1$ through its multiplicity.
\end{warningbox}
\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Using one numerator for the highest power only and omitting the lower powers.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} $\displaystyle \frac{- 2 x^{3} + 8 x^{2} - 18 x + 7}{\left(x^{2} - 3 x + 5\right)^{2}}$

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is repeated irreducible quadratic factor. The appropriate tool is: include a linear numerator over every power of the quadratic.

\textbf{Result.} $\displaystyle \frac{2 - 2 x}{x^{2} - 3 x + 5} + \frac{- 2 x - 3}{\left(x^{2} - 3 x + 5\right)^{2}}$.

\textbf{Check.} Recombine over the highest quadratic power.
\end{examplebox}

\chapter{Mixed Denominator Types}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Combine all partial-fraction template rules.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
The factored denominator contains several factor types or multiplicities.
\section{Procedure}
Classify every factor first; write the entire template before solving any coefficients.

\begin{warningbox}{Template completeness}
Do not skip intermediate powers. A repeated factor contributes a separate numerator over every power from $1$ through its multiplicity.
\end{warningbox}
\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Solving for coefficients before checking that the full mixed template is complete.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} $\displaystyle \frac{- 3 x^{3} - 4 x^{2} + 6 x - 67}{\left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 3\right) \left(x^{2} + 4 x + 7\right)}$

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is mixed linear, repeated, and quadratic factors. The appropriate tool is: classify each factor and write every required numerator and power.

\textbf{Result.} $\displaystyle \frac{3 - 4 x}{x^{2} + 4 x + 7} + \frac{2}{x + 3} - \frac{1}{x - 2}$.

\textbf{Check.} Recombine the full mixed decomposition.
\end{examplebox}

\chapter{Improper Rational Expressions and Partial Fractions}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Decompose rational expressions whose numerator degree is too large.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
The numerator degree is at least the denominator degree.
\section{Procedure}
Use polynomial division first; decompose only the proper remainder fraction.

\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Attempting partial fractions while the rational expression is still improper.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} $\displaystyle \frac{- 3 x^{3} - 6 x^{2} + 4 x - 9}{x \left(x + 3\right)}$

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is improper rational expression. The appropriate tool is: perform polynomial division first, then decompose the proper remainder.

\textbf{Result.} $\displaystyle \left(3 - 3 x\right) - \frac{2}{x + 3} - \frac{3}{x}$.

\textbf{Check.} Combine the polynomial quotient and decomposed remainder.
\end{examplebox}

\chapter{Hybrid Coefficient Methods}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Use the most efficient mixture of coefficient-finding methods.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
Some coefficients isolate at roots while others remain coupled.
\section{Procedure}
Use cover-up or substitution for easy coefficients, then coefficient comparison for the rest; verify at the end.

\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Using one method mechanically when a short substitution can isolate some coefficients immediately.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} Use an efficient mixture of methods to decompose $\displaystyle \frac{8 x^{3} - 16 x^{2} - 39 x - 10}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x - 3\right) \left(x^{2} + 2\right)}$.

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is some coefficients isolate immediately while others do not. The appropriate tool is: use cover-up/substitution for easy constants and coefficient comparison for the rest.

\textbf{Result.} $\displaystyle \frac{5 - 2 x}{x^{2} + 2} + \frac{5}{x - 3} + \frac{5}{x - 4}$.

\textbf{Check.} Recombine all terms.
\end{examplebox}

\chapter{Verifying Decompositions}
\begin{rulebox}{Learning target}
Check a proposed partial-fraction identity and diagnose errors.
\end{rulebox}
\section{Recognize the shape}
A decomposition is supplied rather than requested.
\section{Procedure}
Recombine the right side or clear denominators; compare the resulting numerator with the original numerator.

\begin{warningbox}{Common failure}Checking a proposed identity at only one numerical value. Agreement at one value does not prove an identity.\end{warningbox}
\begin{examplebox}{Worked example}
\textbf{Problem.} Determine whether $\displaystyle \frac{- 2 x - 56}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 6\right)}=\frac{4}{x + 6} - \frac{6}{x - 5}$ is valid.

\textbf{Diagnosis.} The shape is proposed rational identity. The appropriate tool is: recombine over a common denominator or clear denominators and compare.

\textbf{Result.} Valid.

\textbf{Check.} Clear denominators and compare the resulting polynomial identity.
\end{examplebox}
\section{Mastery exercises}
\subsection*{Level I: direct recognition}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item Determine whether $\displaystyle \frac{- x - 26}{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 2\right)}=\frac{3}{x + 2} - \frac{4}{x - 6}$ is valid.
\item Determine whether $\displaystyle \frac{33}{\left(x - 6\right) \left(x + 5\right)}=- \frac{5}{x + 5} + \frac{3}{x - 6}$ is valid.
\item Determine whether $\displaystyle \frac{- 2 x - 56}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 6\right)}=\frac{4}{x + 6} - \frac{6}{x - 5}$ is valid.
\item Determine whether $\displaystyle \frac{- 8 x + 18}{x \left(x - 3\right)}=- \frac{2}{x - 3} - \frac{4}{x}$ is valid.
\item Determine whether $\displaystyle \frac{6 x + 33}{\left(x + 5\right) \left(x + 6\right)}=\frac{3}{x + 6} + \frac{3}{x + 5}$ is valid.
\item Determine whether $\displaystyle \frac{10}{\left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 6\right)}=- \frac{4}{x + 6} + \frac{2}{x + 1}$ is valid.
\item Determine whether $\displaystyle \frac{- 7 x - 16}{\left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 3\right)}=- \frac{5}{x + 3} - \frac{2}{x + 2}$ is valid.
\item Determine whether $\displaystyle \frac{- 8 x - 32}{\left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 7\right)}=- \frac{8}{x + 7} - \frac{2}{x + 3}$ is valid.
\item Determine whether $\displaystyle \frac{5 x + 29}{\left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 5\right)}=- \frac{1}{x + 5} + \frac{6}{x + 1}$ is valid.
\item Determine whether $\displaystyle \frac{6 x - 36}{\left(x - 7\right) \left(x - 4\right)}=\frac{6}{x - 4} + \frac{2}{x - 7}$ is valid.
\end{enumerate}
\subsection*{Level II: routine variations}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{\arabic*.},itemsep=0.65\baselineskip]
\item Determine whether $\displaystyle \frac{- 5 x + 13}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 7\right)}=- \frac{6}{x + 7} + \frac{1}{x - 1}$ is valid.
\item Determine whether $\displaystyle \frac{- 2 x + 10}{\left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right)}=- \frac{6}{x + 1} + \frac{3}{x - 1}$ is valid.
\item Determine whether $\displaystyle \frac{2 x - 4}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x - 4\right)}=- \frac{4}{x - 4} + \frac{6}{x - 5}$ is valid.
\item Determine whether $\displaystyle \frac{7 x + 2}{x \left(x + 1\right)}=\frac{4}{x + 1} + \frac{2}{x}$ is valid.
\item Determine whether $\displaystyle \frac{11 x - 19}{\left(x - 4\right) \left(x + 1\right)}=\frac{6}{x + 1} + \frac{5}{x - 4}$ is valid.
\item Determine whether $\displaystyle \frac{7 x + 36}{\left(x + 5\right) \left(x + 6\right)}=\frac{8}{x + 6} + \frac{1}{x + 5}$ is valid.
\item Determine whether $\displaystyle \frac{- 12 x + 18}{\left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 2\right)}=- \frac{6}{x + 2} - \frac{6}{x - 5}$ is valid.
\item Determine whether $\displaystyle \frac{x + 7}{\left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 4\right)}=- \frac{2}{x + 4} + \frac{4}{x + 3}$ is valid.
\item Determine whether $\displaystyle \frac{x + 5}{\left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 4\right)}=- \frac{1}{x + 4} + \frac{2}{x + 3}$ is valid.
\item Determine whether $\displaystyle \frac{2 x + 7}{\left(x + 1\right) \left(x + 2\right)}=- \frac{1}{x + 2} + \frac{5}{x + 1}$ is valid.
\end{enumerate}

\part{Part V: Tool Selection Capstone}
\chapter{Systematic Decision Process}
\begin{rulebox}{Classification before calculation}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*]
\item Is the task factorization, a rational-expression operation, polynomial division, or partial-fraction decomposition?
\item Is there a greatest common factor or leading negative sign?
\item Which factoring shape is present: squares, cubes, trinomial, grouping, or substitution pattern?
\item For a rational operation, what values are excluded by the original expression?
\item For polynomial division, is the divisor exactly $x-c$? If yes, synthetic division is available; otherwise use long division.
\item For partial fractions, is the expression proper? If not, divide first.
\item Is the denominator completely factored? Classify every factor as distinct/repeated linear or distinct/repeated irreducible quadratic.
\item Write the complete template before determining coefficients.
\item Choose coefficient tools: cover-up, strategic substitution, equating coefficients, or a hybrid.
\item Verify by multiplication, recombination, or the identity $P=DQ+R$.
\end{enumerate}
\end{rulebox}
\begin{warningbox}{Do not let the operation sign hide the structure}
The visible symbol $+$, $-$, $\times$, or $\div$ tells you the requested operation, but the internal polynomial shapes determine the tools required to complete it.
\end{warningbox}
\chapter{Capstone: Identify, Select, Execute, Verify}
For every problem, record four items before doing the algebra:
\[
\text{Shape}\qquad\text{Tool(s)}\qquad\text{Order of use}\qquad\text{Verification method}.
\]
No problem is labeled by technique. Some require one tool; others require several in sequence.

\part{Part VI: Reference and Answer Key}
\chapter{Formula and Procedure Reference}
\begin{rulebox}{Core factoring identities}
\[
A^2-B^2=(A-B)(A+B),
\]
\[
A^2\pm2AB+B^2=(A\pm B)^2,
\]
\[
A^3-B^3=(A-B)(A^2+AB+B^2),\qquad
A^3+B^3=(A+B)(A^2-AB+B^2).
\]
\end{rulebox}
\begin{rulebox}{Polynomial division}
For nonzero $D(x)$ there are unique polynomials $Q(x),R(x)$ such that
\[
P(x)=D(x)Q(x)+R(x),\qquad \deg R<\deg D.
\]
\end{rulebox}
\begin{rulebox}{Partial-fraction templates}
\begin{align*}
(x-r)^m&:\quad \frac{A_1}{x-r}+\frac{A_2}{(x-r)^2}+\cdots+\frac{A_m}{(x-r)^m},\\
q(x)^m&:\quad \frac{A_1x+B_1}{q(x)}+\frac{A_2x+B_2}{q(x)^2}+\cdots+\frac{A_mx+B_m}{q(x)^m},
\end{align*}
where $q$ is irreducible quadratic. Add the contributions from every distinct denominator factor.
\end{rulebox}
\chapter{Answer Key}
Answers are intentionally concise, as in the companion division workbook. Rework any missed problem from its recognition cue rather than memorizing the final form.

\section*{A. Greatest Common Factor and Sign Extraction}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{A. Greatest Common Factor and Sign Extraction}
\begin{multicols}{2}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{A\arabic*.},itemsep=0.45\baselineskip]
\item $ 12 x^{2} \left(2 x + 5\right) $
\item $ 3 x \left(2 x + 5\right) $
\item $ 6 x^{2} \left(7 x + 11\right) $
\item $ - 5 x \left(7 x^{2} - 10\right) $
\item $ 8 x^{2} \left(5 x - 11\right) $
\item $ 4 x^{2} \left(9 x + 4\right) $
\item $ - 6 x^{2} \left(2 x^{2} + 9\right) $

\section*{K. Multiplication of Rational Expressions}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{K. Multiplication of Rational Expressions}
\begin{multicols}{2}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{K\arabic*.},itemsep=0.45\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 11 x + 28}{x^{2} + 2 x}$, with $x\ne -2,\,0,\,2,\,8$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 7 x + 10}{x^{2} - x - 56}$, with $x\ne -7,\,-5,\,-4,\,8$.

\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 8 x + 12}{x^{2} + 7 x + 6}$, with $x\ne -6,\,-5,\,-2,\,-1$.

\item $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 4 x - 32}{x^{2} + 13 x + 42}$, with $x\ne -7,\,-6,\,-3,\,1$.

\item $\displaystyle \frac{- 2 x - 5}{\left(x^{2} - 2 x + 2\right)^{2}} + \frac{3 x - 3}{x^{2} - 2 x + 2} + \frac{5}{x + 4} + \frac{1}{\left(x + 4\right)^{2}} - \frac{1}{x - 6}$.

\item $\displaystyle \frac{- 4 x - 3}{\left(x^{2} - 2 x + 4\right)^{2}} + \frac{3 x + 3}{x^{2} - 2 x + 4} + \frac{5}{x + 1} + \frac{2}{x - 2} - \frac{1}{\left(x - 2\right)^{2}}$.

\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{Z\arabic*.},itemsep=0.45\baselineskip]

\item $\displaystyle \left(2 x^{3} + 3 x + 1\right) - \frac{1}{x + 3} - \frac{4}{x - 2} + \frac{5}{x - 5}$.
\item $\displaystyle \left(2 x^{3} + 2 x^{2} - 3\right) + \frac{4}{x - 1} - \frac{2}{x - 4} - \frac{5}{x}$.
\item $\displaystyle \left(- 2 x^{3} - 2 x^{2} + 2 x + 3\right) + \frac{2}{x - 3} - \frac{4}{x - 6} + \frac{4}{x}$.
\item $\displaystyle \left(- x^{3} - 2 x^{2} + x\right) - \frac{3}{x + 4} - \frac{1}{x - 6} + \frac{4}{x}$.
\item $\displaystyle \left(x^{3} - 3 x^{2} + 2 x - 2\right) + \frac{4}{x + 4} + \frac{3}{x + 2} + \frac{5}{x - 3}$.
\item $\displaystyle \left(- x^{3} + x^{2} + x - 3\right) + \frac{5}{x - 3} - \frac{3}{x - 5} + \frac{1}{x}$.
\item $\displaystyle \left(3 x^{3} - x^{2} + x - 3\right) + \frac{5}{x + 3} - \frac{5}{x - 1} + \frac{5}{x - 3}$.
\item $\displaystyle \left(2 x^{3} - x^{2} - x + 3\right) + \frac{2}{x + 1} + \frac{1}{x - 4} - \frac{1}{x - 5}$.
\item $\displaystyle \left(- 2 x^{3} - 2 x^{2} + 2 x + 3\right) - \frac{1}{x - 3} + \frac{5}{x - 4} + \frac{4}{x - 5}$.
\item $\displaystyle \left(x^{3} - x^{2} + 3 x\right) - \frac{2}{x + 2} + \frac{2}{x - 3} - \frac{3}{x - 6}$.
\end{enumerate}

\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{AA. Hybrid Coefficient Methods}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{AA\arabic*.},itemsep=0.45\baselineskip]
\item $\displaystyle \frac{- 4 x - 4}{x^{2} + x + 7} - \frac{1}{x + 4} - \frac{4}{x - 4}$.

\item $\displaystyle \frac{x - 3}{x^{2} + x + 8} + \frac{1}{x - 3} + \frac{5}{x - 5}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{4 - 4 x}{x^{2} + 4 x + 5} + \frac{5}{x - 4} + \frac{2}{x - 6}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{4 x - 3}{x^{2} + 3 x + 3} - \frac{3}{x - 4} + \frac{2}{x - 5}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{5 - 3 x}{x^{2} - 3 x + 3} + \frac{3}{x + 3} - \frac{1}{x - 3}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{4 - 2 x}{x^{2} + 1} - \frac{4}{x + 6} - \frac{4}{x - 1}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{2 x - 2}{x^{2} - x + 5} + \frac{5}{x - 2} + \frac{3}{x - 3}$.

\item $\displaystyle \frac{5 - 4 x}{x^{2} - x + 5} - \frac{4}{x + 4} + \frac{2}{\left(x + 4\right)^{2}} + \frac{4}{x - 4}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x + 5}{x^{2} - 2 x + 2} - \frac{2}{x + 3} - \frac{4}{x - 5} + \frac{1}{\left(x - 5\right)^{2}}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{- x - 4}{x^{2} - 4 x + 7} + \frac{4}{x + 6} + \frac{2}{\left(x + 6\right)^{2}} + \frac{5}{x - 4}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{4 - 2 x}{x^{2} - 5 x + 8} + \frac{2}{x - 4} - \frac{5}{x} + \frac{2}{x^{2}}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{2 - 4 x}{x^{2} + x + 1} + \frac{3}{x + 3} + \frac{2}{\left(x + 3\right)^{2}} - \frac{3}{x - 3}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{- 3 x - 4}{x^{2} - 3 x + 8} - \frac{3}{x + 3} + \frac{3}{x + 2} + \frac{3}{\left(x + 2\right)^{2}}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{2 x - 3}{x^{2} + x + 8} - \frac{4}{x + 5} + \frac{1}{x - 2} + \frac{1}{\left(x - 2\right)^{2}}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{3 - 4 x}{x^{2} - 2 x + 5} + \frac{2}{x + 2} - \frac{1}{\left(x + 2\right)^{2}} + \frac{1}{x - 4}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{- 3 x - 1}{x^{2} + 4 x + 7} + \frac{3}{x - 6} + \frac{3}{\left(x - 6\right)^{2}} - \frac{2}{x}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{2 - 3 x}{x^{2} + 3 x + 8} + \frac{5}{x + 2} + \frac{5}{x - 3} + \frac{3}{\left(x - 3\right)^{2}}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{4 x + 5}{x^{2} - x + 5} - \frac{1}{x + 4} - \frac{1}{x - 5} - \frac{1}{\left(x - 5\right)^{2}}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{- 2 x - 4}{x^{2} - 3 x + 5} - \frac{5}{x + 2} - \frac{4}{x + 1} - \frac{3}{\left(x + 1\right)^{2}}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{- x - 5}{x^{2} + x + 7} + \frac{3}{x + 1} - \frac{1}{x - 1} + \frac{2}{\left(x - 1\right)^{2}}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x - 5}{x^{2} + 3 x + 3} - \frac{5}{x + 3} + \frac{3}{x - 6} + \frac{1}{\left(x - 6\right)^{2}}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x + 1}{x^{2} - 2 x + 8} + \frac{2}{x + 5} - \frac{5}{x - 6} - \frac{5}{\left(x - 6\right)^{2}}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{- 2 x - 4}{x^{2} + 1} - \frac{4}{x + 2} - \frac{1}{x - 5} + \frac{3}{\left(x - 5\right)^{2}}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{2 x + 2}{x^{2} - 2 x + 4} - \frac{2}{x + 3} - \frac{1}{\left(x + 3\right)^{2}} + \frac{4}{x - 4}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{- 3 x - 5}{x^{2} - x + 5} - \frac{3}{x + 6} + \frac{5}{x - 5} + \frac{1}{\left(x - 5\right)^{2}}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{2 x - 2}{x^{2} + 2 x + 2} - \frac{1}{x + 5} + \frac{3}{\left(x + 5\right)^{2}} + \frac{2}{x - 3}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{4 - 4 x}{x^{2} + 5 x + 8} + \frac{4}{x + 6} + \frac{2}{\left(x + 6\right)^{2}} - \frac{2}{x - 1}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{3 x + 3}{x^{2} - 3 x + 4} - \frac{5}{x - 3} + \frac{3}{x - 5} - \frac{1}{\left(x - 5\right)^{2}}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{2 x - 5}{x^{2} + 3 x + 5} + \frac{5}{x + 1} - \frac{4}{\left(x + 1\right)^{2}} - \frac{4}{x - 2}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{x + 1}{x^{2} + x + 6} + \frac{3}{x + 5} - \frac{2}{x + 1} + \frac{2}{\left(x + 1\right)^{2}}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{3 x + 3}{x^{2} - x + 3} - \frac{3}{x - 5} + \frac{3}{\left(x - 5\right)^{2}} - \frac{5}{x - 6}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{4 x - 4}{x^{2} - x + 4} - \frac{3}{x - 5} + \frac{1}{x - 6} - \frac{5}{\left(x - 6\right)^{2}}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{2 x + 2}{x^{2} - x + 6} + \frac{5}{x + 6} - \frac{3}{x + 5} - \frac{2}{\left(x + 5\right)^{2}}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{2 x - 3}{x^{2} + 3} - \frac{2}{x - 5} + \frac{1}{\left(x - 5\right)^{2}} - \frac{3}{x}$.
\item $\displaystyle \frac{3 - 2 x}{x^{2} - 2 x + 3} - \frac{3}{x + 4} - \frac{4}{x - 1} + \frac{2}{\left(x - 1\right)^{2}}$.
\end{enumerate}
\section*{AB. Verifying Decompositions}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{AB. Verifying Decompositions}

\item Valid.

\item Valid.

\item Valid.

\item Valid.

\item Valid.

\item Valid.

\item Valid.

\item Valid.
\item Invalid. The corrected decomposition is $\displaystyle \frac{1}{x + 6} + \frac{4}{x + 4} + \frac{2}{x - 1}$.
\item Valid.

\item Valid.
\item Invalid. The corrected decomposition is $\displaystyle \frac{3}{x + 4} + \frac{3}{x - 2} - \frac{3}{x - 3}$.
\item Valid.
