\section*{CAP. Capstone}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{CAP. Capstone}
\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=*,label=\textbf{CAP\arabic*.},itemsep=0.45\baselineskip]
\item \textbf{Shape:} common numerical/monomial factor. \textbf{Tools:} extract the greatest common factor. \textbf{Result:} $ 12 x^{2} \left(2 x + 5\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} difference of two perfect squares. \textbf{Tools:} apply $A^2-B^2=(A-B)(A+B)$ repeatedly. \textbf{Result:} $ 9 \left(2 x - 3\right) \left(2 x + 3\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} three-term perfect-square pattern. \textbf{Tools:} match $A^2\pm2AB+B^2$. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(4 x - 1\right)^{2} $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} sum or difference of cubes. \textbf{Tools:} apply the cube identity, after any GCF. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(2 x + 3\right) \left(4 x^{2} - 6 x + 9\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} monic quadratic trinomial. \textbf{Tools:} find two integers with sum $-b$ and product $c$. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(x - 4\right) \left(x - 2\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} nonmonic quadratic trinomial. \textbf{Tools:} split the middle term or use an $ac$ method, then group. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(x + 6\right) \left(3 x + 8\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} four or more terms with a repeated binomial after grouping. \textbf{Tools:} group terms and extract the common binomial. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(x - 5\right) \left(2 x^{2} + 2 x - 1\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} quadratic pattern in $x^2$. \textbf{Tools:} substitute $u=x^2$, factor in $u$, then replace $u$. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(x^{2} + 1\right) \left(x^{2} + 7\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} expression containing more than one factoring pattern. \textbf{Tools:} GCF first, then repeatedly factor until no listed tool applies. \textbf{Result:} $ 2 x \left(x - 2\right)^{2} \left(x + 2\right) \left(x^{2} + 2 x + 4\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} quotient with common polynomial factors. \textbf{Tools:} factor both parts, cancel factors only, preserve original restrictions. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{x + 4}{x + 5}$, with $x\ne -5,\,-3$. \textbf{Check:} recombine or substitute an allowed numerical value, while checking all original restrictions.
\item \textbf{Shape:} product of rational expressions. \textbf{Tools:} factor, cancel across the product, then multiply. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 11 x + 28}{x^{2} + 2 x}$, with $x\ne -2,\,0,\,2,\,8$. \textbf{Check:} recombine or substitute an allowed numerical value, while checking all original restrictions.
\item \textbf{Shape:} quotient of rational expressions. \textbf{Tools:} record restrictions, multiply by the reciprocal, factor, and cancel. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 12 x + 32}{x^{2} + 2 x - 24}$, with $x\ne -8,\,-6,\,-1,\,0,\,4$. \textbf{Check:} recombine or substitute an allowed numerical value, while checking all original restrictions.
\item \textbf{Shape:} sum or difference with unlike denominators. \textbf{Tools:} factor denominators, build the LCD, combine, and simplify. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{- 10 x + 48}{x^{2} - 9 x + 18}$, with $x\ne 3,\,6$. \textbf{Check:} recombine or substitute an allowed numerical value, while checking all original restrictions.
\item \textbf{Shape:} fraction whose numerator or denominator contains fractions. \textbf{Tools:} identify the internal LCD and clear all small denominators. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{3 x - 7}{x - 1}$, with $x\ne 1,\,3$. \textbf{Check:} recombine or substitute an allowed numerical value, while checking all original restrictions.
\item \textbf{Shape:} polynomial dividend and nonzero polynomial divisor. \textbf{Tools:} polynomial long division with zero placeholders as needed. \textbf{Result:} $Q(x)=- 3 x^{2} - 2 x + 1$, $R(x)=1$. \textbf{Check:} check $P(x)=D(x)Q(x)+R(x)$ and $\deg R<\deg D$.
\item \textbf{Shape:} linear divisor of the form $x-c$. \textbf{Tools:} synthetic division using $c$ and every coefficient, including zeros. \textbf{Result:} $Q(x)=- 2 x^{3} - x^{2} - 4 x - 3$, $R=7$. \textbf{Check:} check $P(x)=D(x)Q(x)+R(x)$ and $\deg R<\deg D$.
\item \textbf{Shape:} inspect the divisor. \textbf{Tools:} use synthetic division only for $x-c$; otherwise use long division. \textbf{Result:} polynomial long division; $Q(x)=- 3 x - 4$, $R(x)=3 x + 2$. \textbf{Check:} check $P(x)=D(x)Q(x)+R(x)$ and $\deg R<\deg D$.
\item \textbf{Shape:} proper rational expression with distinct linear denominator factors. \textbf{Tools:} write the distinct-linear template and determine its constants. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle - \frac{6}{x + 3} + \frac{3}{x - 8}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} one or more repeated linear factors. \textbf{Tools:} include a term for every power from $1$ through the multiplicity. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle - \frac{6}{x + 2} - \frac{6}{\left(x + 2\right)^{2}}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} distinct irreducible quadratic factors. \textbf{Tools:} place a general linear numerator over each quadratic factor. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{- 4 x - 4}{x^{2} + 2} + \frac{- 3 x - 2}{x^{2} + 2 x + 4}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} repeated irreducible quadratic factor. \textbf{Tools:} include a linear numerator over every power of the quadratic. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{5 - 3 x}{\left(x^{2} - 4 x + 5\right)^{2}} + \frac{- 2 x - 4}{x^{2} - 4 x + 5}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} mixed linear, repeated, and quadratic factors. \textbf{Tools:} classify each factor and write every required numerator and power. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{4 - 4 x}{x^{2} + 2 x + 8} - \frac{3}{x + 6} - \frac{5}{x + 1} - \frac{5}{\left(x + 1\right)^{2}}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} improper rational expression. \textbf{Tools:} perform polynomial division first, then decompose the proper remainder. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle 2 x + \frac{3}{x - 4} - \frac{1}{x - 6}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} common numerical/monomial factor. \textbf{Tools:} extract the greatest common factor. \textbf{Result:} $ 12 x^{3} \left(x^{2} - 8\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} difference of two perfect squares. \textbf{Tools:} apply $A^2-B^2=(A-B)(A+B)$ repeatedly. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(2 x^{3} - 5\right) \left(2 x^{3} + 5\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} three-term perfect-square pattern. \textbf{Tools:} match $A^2\pm2AB+B^2$. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(x - 5\right)^{2} $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} sum or difference of cubes. \textbf{Tools:} apply the cube identity, after any GCF. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(x - 6\right) \left(x^{2} + 6 x + 36\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} monic quadratic trinomial. \textbf{Tools:} find two integers with sum $-b$ and product $c$. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 7\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} nonmonic quadratic trinomial. \textbf{Tools:} split the middle term or use an $ac$ method, then group. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(3 x - 1\right) \left(3 x + 7\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} four or more terms with a repeated binomial after grouping. \textbf{Tools:} group terms and extract the common binomial. \textbf{Result:} $ - \left(4 x + 1\right) \left(2 x^{2} - 7\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} quadratic pattern in $x^2$. \textbf{Tools:} substitute $u=x^2$, factor in $u$, then replace $u$. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(x^{2} - 7\right) \left(x^{2} + 4\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} expression containing more than one factoring pattern. \textbf{Tools:} GCF first, then repeatedly factor until no listed tool applies. \textbf{Result:} $ 5 x \left(x - 1\right)^{2} \left(x + 1\right) \left(x^{2} + x + 1\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} quotient with common polynomial factors. \textbf{Tools:} factor both parts, cancel factors only, preserve original restrictions. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{x - 3}{x + 2}$, with $x\ne -7,\,-2$. \textbf{Check:} recombine or substitute an allowed numerical value, while checking all original restrictions.
\item \textbf{Shape:} product of rational expressions. \textbf{Tools:} factor, cancel across the product, then multiply. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 2 x - 48}{x^{2} - 8 x + 7}$, with $x\ne -7,\,1,\,2,\,7$. \textbf{Check:} recombine or substitute an allowed numerical value, while checking all original restrictions.
\item \textbf{Shape:} quotient of rational expressions. \textbf{Tools:} record restrictions, multiply by the reciprocal, factor, and cancel. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 9 x + 8}{x^{2} - 5 x + 4}$, with $x\ne -8,\,1,\,3,\,4,\,8$. \textbf{Check:} recombine or substitute an allowed numerical value, while checking all original restrictions.
\item \textbf{Shape:} sum or difference with unlike denominators. \textbf{Tools:} factor denominators, build the LCD, combine, and simplify. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{2 x - 24}{x^{2} - 14 x + 48}$, with $x\ne 6,\,8$. \textbf{Check:} recombine or substitute an allowed numerical value, while checking all original restrictions.
\item \textbf{Shape:} fraction whose numerator or denominator contains fractions. \textbf{Tools:} identify the internal LCD and clear all small denominators. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{x + 3}{x - 1}$, with $x\ne -1,\,1$. \textbf{Check:} recombine or substitute an allowed numerical value, while checking all original restrictions.
\item \textbf{Shape:} polynomial dividend and nonzero polynomial divisor. \textbf{Tools:} polynomial long division with zero placeholders as needed. \textbf{Result:} $Q(x)=- 3 x^{2} + 4 x + 3$, $R(x)=3$. \textbf{Check:} check $P(x)=D(x)Q(x)+R(x)$ and $\deg R<\deg D$.
\item \textbf{Shape:} linear divisor of the form $x-c$. \textbf{Tools:} synthetic division using $c$ and every coefficient, including zeros. \textbf{Result:} $Q(x)=x^{4} + 2 x^{3} - 4 x^{2} + 3 x + 1$, $R=-8$. \textbf{Check:} check $P(x)=D(x)Q(x)+R(x)$ and $\deg R<\deg D$.
\item \textbf{Shape:} inspect the divisor. \textbf{Tools:} use synthetic division only for $x-c$; otherwise use long division. \textbf{Result:} synthetic division (or long division); $Q(x)=2 x + 2$, $R(x)=-1$. \textbf{Check:} check $P(x)=D(x)Q(x)+R(x)$ and $\deg R<\deg D$.
\item \textbf{Shape:} proper rational expression with distinct linear denominator factors. \textbf{Tools:} write the distinct-linear template and determine its constants. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{7}{x + 4} + \frac{4}{x + 2} - \frac{7}{x - 6}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} one or more repeated linear factors. \textbf{Tools:} include a term for every power from $1$ through the multiplicity. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle - \frac{5}{x - 4} - \frac{6}{\left(x - 4\right)^{2}} - \frac{5}{\left(x - 4\right)^{3}}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} distinct irreducible quadratic factors. \textbf{Tools:} place a general linear numerator over each quadratic factor. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{- 5 x - 1}{x^{2} + 4 x + 5} + \frac{- 4 x - 3}{x^{2} - 4 x + 8}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} repeated irreducible quadratic factor. \textbf{Tools:} include a linear numerator over every power of the quadratic. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{4 x - 3}{\left(x^{2} + 8\right)^{2}} + \frac{4 x - 2}{x^{2} + 8}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} mixed linear, repeated, and quadratic factors. \textbf{Tools:} classify each factor and write every required numerator and power. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{4 x - 1}{x^{2} + 8} - \frac{4}{x + 6} - \frac{1}{\left(x + 6\right)^{2}} + \frac{2}{x - 5}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} improper rational expression. \textbf{Tools:} perform polynomial division first, then decompose the proper remainder. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \left(- 2 x^{2} + 2 x - 2\right) - \frac{4}{x + 2} - \frac{1}{x - 6}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} common numerical/monomial factor. \textbf{Tools:} extract the greatest common factor. \textbf{Result:} $ - 5 x^{4} \left(7 x^{4} + 8 x^{2} - 7\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} difference of two perfect squares. \textbf{Tools:} apply $A^2-B^2=(A-B)(A+B)$ repeatedly. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(x + 2\right) \left(x + 4\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} three-term perfect-square pattern. \textbf{Tools:} match $A^2\pm2AB+B^2$. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(4 x^{2} + 3\right)^{2} $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} sum or difference of cubes. \textbf{Tools:} apply the cube identity, after any GCF. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(4 x - 3\right) \left(16 x^{2} + 12 x + 9\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} monic quadratic trinomial. \textbf{Tools:} find two integers with sum $-b$ and product $c$. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(x - 6\right) \left(x - 4\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} nonmonic quadratic trinomial. \textbf{Tools:} split the middle term or use an $ac$ method, then group. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(2 x + 3\right) \left(3 x + 1\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} four or more terms with a repeated binomial after grouping. \textbf{Tools:} group terms and extract the common binomial. \textbf{Result:} $ 2 \left(2 x + 1\right) \left(2 x^{2} - 4 x - 1\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} quadratic pattern in $x^3$. \textbf{Tools:} substitute $u=x^3$, factor in $u$, then replace $u$. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(x + 1\right) \left(x^{3} + 5\right) \left(x^{2} - x + 1\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} expression containing more than one factoring pattern. \textbf{Tools:} GCF first, then repeatedly factor until no listed tool applies. \textbf{Result:} $ - 6 x^{3} \left(x - 2\right)^{2} \left(x + 2\right) \left(x^{2} + 2 x + 4\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} quotient with common polynomial factors. \textbf{Tools:} factor both parts, cancel factors only, preserve original restrictions. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{x - 3}{x - 5}$, with $x\ne -1,\,5,\,6$. \textbf{Check:} recombine or substitute an allowed numerical value, while checking all original restrictions.
\item \textbf{Shape:} product of rational expressions. \textbf{Tools:} factor, cancel across the product, then multiply. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 7 x - 8}{x^{2} + 5 x + 6}$, with $x\ne -5,\,-3,\,-2,\,4$. \textbf{Check:} recombine or substitute an allowed numerical value, while checking all original restrictions.
\item \textbf{Shape:} quotient of rational expressions. \textbf{Tools:} record restrictions, multiply by the reciprocal, factor, and cancel. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 10 x + 21}{x^{2} - 2 x - 24}$, with $x\ne -7,\,-5,\,-4,\,2,\,6$. \textbf{Check:} recombine or substitute an allowed numerical value, while checking all original restrictions.
\item \textbf{Shape:} sum or difference with unlike denominators. \textbf{Tools:} factor denominators, build the LCD, combine, and simplify. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{8 x - 14}{x^{3} - 12 x - 16}$, with $x\ne -2,\,4$. \textbf{Check:} recombine or substitute an allowed numerical value, while checking all original restrictions.
\item \textbf{Shape:} fraction whose numerator or denominator contains fractions. \textbf{Tools:} identify the internal LCD and clear all small denominators. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{- 2 x + 24}{x + 6}$, with $x\ne -6,\,3$. \textbf{Check:} recombine or substitute an allowed numerical value, while checking all original restrictions.
\item \textbf{Shape:} polynomial dividend and nonzero polynomial divisor. \textbf{Tools:} polynomial long division with zero placeholders as needed. \textbf{Result:} $Q(x)=x^{2} - 3 x + 1$, $R(x)=4$. \textbf{Check:} check $P(x)=D(x)Q(x)+R(x)$ and $\deg R<\deg D$.
\item \textbf{Shape:} linear divisor of the form $x-c$. \textbf{Tools:} synthetic division using $c$ and every coefficient, including zeros. \textbf{Result:} $Q(x)=- 2 x^{4} + 2 x^{2} + 5$, $R=0$. \textbf{Check:} check $P(x)=D(x)Q(x)+R(x)$ and $\deg R<\deg D$.
\item \textbf{Shape:} inspect the divisor. \textbf{Tools:} use synthetic division only for $x-c$; otherwise use long division. \textbf{Result:} synthetic division (or long division); $Q(x)=x^{5} + 4 x^{4} - 2 x^{2} + 4 x + 4$, $R(x)=-3$. \textbf{Check:} check $P(x)=D(x)Q(x)+R(x)$ and $\deg R<\deg D$.
\item \textbf{Shape:} proper rational expression with distinct linear denominator factors. \textbf{Tools:} write the distinct-linear template and determine its constants. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{7}{x + 6} + \frac{6}{x - 2} + \frac{2}{x - 8}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} one or more repeated linear factors. \textbf{Tools:} include a term for every power from $1$ through the multiplicity. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{3}{x + 7} - \frac{1}{x - 5} + \frac{2}{\left(x - 5\right)^{2}} + \frac{6}{\left(x - 5\right)^{3}}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} distinct irreducible quadratic factors. \textbf{Tools:} place a general linear numerator over each quadratic factor. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{5 x + 1}{x^{2} + 3 x + 3} + \frac{5 x + 2}{x^{2} + 4 x + 7}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} repeated irreducible quadratic factor. \textbf{Tools:} include a linear numerator over every power of the quadratic. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{4 - x}{\left(x^{2} - 4 x + 7\right)^{2}} + \frac{2 x - 2}{\left(x^{2} - 4 x + 7\right)^{3}} + \frac{3 x + 1}{x^{2} - 4 x + 7}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} mixed linear, repeated, and quadratic factors. \textbf{Tools:} classify each factor and write every required numerator and power. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{5 - 4 x}{x^{2} - 5 x + 8} + \frac{- 2 x - 5}{\left(x^{2} - 5 x + 8\right)^{2}} + \frac{2}{x + 2} + \frac{2}{x - 4} - \frac{2}{\left(x - 4\right)^{2}}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} improper rational expression. \textbf{Tools:} perform polynomial division first, then decompose the proper remainder. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \left(- 2 x^{2} - 2 x + 1\right) + \frac{2}{x + 3} + \frac{2}{x + 1} - \frac{4}{x}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} common numerical/monomial factor. \textbf{Tools:} extract the greatest common factor. \textbf{Result:} $ - 12 x^{8} \left(x - 2\right) \left(2 x + 3\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} difference of two perfect squares. \textbf{Tools:} apply $A^2-B^2=(A-B)(A+B)$ repeatedly. \textbf{Result:} $ 6 x^{3} \left(x - 2\right) \left(x + 2\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} three-term perfect-square pattern. \textbf{Tools:} match $A^2\pm2AB+B^2$. \textbf{Result:} $ 196 x \left(x - 1\right)^{2} $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} sum or difference of cubes. \textbf{Tools:} apply the cube identity, after any GCF. \textbf{Result:} $ 2 x^{2} \left(x + 2\right) \left(x^{2} - 2 x + 4\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} monic quadratic trinomial. \textbf{Tools:} find two integers with sum $-b$ and product $c$. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(x + 3\right) \left(x + 7\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} nonmonic quadratic trinomial. \textbf{Tools:} split the middle term or use an $ac$ method, then group. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(3 x - 5\right) \left(3 x + 4\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} four or more terms with a repeated binomial after grouping. \textbf{Tools:} group terms and extract the common binomial. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(x - 1\right) \left(6 x^{2} + 6 x - 1\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} quadratic pattern in $x^2$. \textbf{Tools:} substitute $u=x^2$, factor in $u$, then replace $u$. \textbf{Result:} $ 3 x^{2} \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right) \left(x^{2} + 4\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} expression containing more than one factoring pattern. \textbf{Tools:} GCF first, then repeatedly factor until no listed tool applies. \textbf{Result:} $ - 3 x \left(x - 1\right)^{2} \left(x + 1\right) \left(x^{2} + x + 1\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} quotient with common polynomial factors. \textbf{Tools:} factor both parts, cancel factors only, preserve original restrictions. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{x^{3} + 3 x^{2} + x + 3}{x + 5}$, with $x\ne -5,\,-1,\,2$. \textbf{Check:} recombine or substitute an allowed numerical value, while checking all original restrictions.
\item \textbf{Shape:} product of rational expressions. \textbf{Tools:} factor, cancel across the product, then multiply. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} + 8 x + 12}{x^{2} + 2 x - 48}$, with $x\ne -8,\,-5,\,5,\,6$. \textbf{Check:} recombine or substitute an allowed numerical value, while checking all original restrictions.
\item \textbf{Shape:} quotient of rational expressions. \textbf{Tools:} record restrictions, multiply by the reciprocal, factor, and cancel. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{x^{2} - 7 x + 6}{x^{2} - 3 x - 10}$, with $x\ne -4,\,-2,\,5,\,6,\,7$. \textbf{Check:} recombine or substitute an allowed numerical value, while checking all original restrictions.
\item \textbf{Shape:} sum or difference with unlike denominators. \textbf{Tools:} factor denominators, build the LCD, combine, and simplify. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{- 4 x + 13}{x^{4} - 11 x^{3} + 29 x^{2} - 11 x + 28}$, with $x\ne 4,\,7$. \textbf{Check:} recombine or substitute an allowed numerical value, while checking all original restrictions.
\item \textbf{Shape:} fraction whose numerator or denominator contains fractions. \textbf{Tools:} identify the internal LCD and clear all small denominators. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle - \frac{x}{4} + \frac{1}{2}$, with $x\ne -2,\,6$. \textbf{Check:} recombine or substitute an allowed numerical value, while checking all original restrictions.
\item \textbf{Shape:} polynomial dividend and nonzero polynomial divisor. \textbf{Tools:} polynomial long division with zero placeholders as needed. \textbf{Result:} $Q(x)=- 3 x$, $R(x)=3$. \textbf{Check:} check $P(x)=D(x)Q(x)+R(x)$ and $\deg R<\deg D$.
\item \textbf{Shape:} linear divisor of the form $x-c$. \textbf{Tools:} synthetic division using $c$ and every coefficient, including zeros. \textbf{Result:} $Q(x)=5 x^{4} - x^{3} + x^{2} + 5 x - 1$, $R=1$. \textbf{Check:} check $P(x)=D(x)Q(x)+R(x)$ and $\deg R<\deg D$.
\item \textbf{Shape:} inspect the divisor. \textbf{Tools:} use synthetic division only for $x-c$; otherwise use long division. \textbf{Result:} polynomial long division; $Q(x)=x^{5} + 3 x^{4} - 4 x^{3} + 3 x - 2$, $R(x)=-4$. \textbf{Check:} check $P(x)=D(x)Q(x)+R(x)$ and $\deg R<\deg D$.
\item \textbf{Shape:} proper rational expression with distinct linear denominator factors. \textbf{Tools:} write the distinct-linear template and determine its constants. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{2}{x + 5} - \frac{2}{x - 1} - \frac{4}{x - 6} + \frac{3}{x - 8}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} one or more repeated linear factors. \textbf{Tools:} include a term for every power from $1$ through the multiplicity. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle - \frac{3}{x - 4} + \frac{4}{\left(x - 4\right)^{2}} + \frac{1}{\left(x - 4\right)^{3}} + \frac{3}{\left(x - 4\right)^{4}} + \frac{5}{x - 5}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} distinct irreducible quadratic factors. \textbf{Tools:} place a general linear numerator over each quadratic factor. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{1 - 2 x}{x^{2} - 5 x + 8} + \frac{4 x - 3}{x^{2} - 3 x + 7}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} repeated irreducible quadratic factor. \textbf{Tools:} include a linear numerator over every power of the quadratic. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{2 x - 4}{x^{2} + 5 x + 7} + \frac{3 x - 1}{\left(x^{2} + 5 x + 7\right)^{3}} + \frac{4 x - 1}{\left(x^{2} + 5 x + 7\right)^{2}} - \frac{4}{x - 4}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} mixed linear, repeated, and quadratic factors. \textbf{Tools:} classify each factor and write every required numerator and power. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \frac{3 - 3 x}{x^{2} - 4 x + 7} + \frac{2 x - 4}{\left(x^{2} - 4 x + 7\right)^{2}} + \frac{2}{x + 5} - \frac{3}{x + 3} + \frac{3}{\left(x + 3\right)^{2}}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} improper rational expression. \textbf{Tools:} perform polynomial division first, then decompose the proper remainder. \textbf{Result:} $\displaystyle \left(2 x^{3} - x^{2} - x + 3\right) + \frac{2}{x + 1} + \frac{1}{x - 4} - \frac{1}{x - 5}$. \textbf{Check:} recombine the decomposition over the original denominator and compare numerators.
\item \textbf{Shape:} common numerical/monomial factor. \textbf{Tools:} extract the greatest common factor. \textbf{Result:} $ 8 x \left(x - 9\right) \left(7 x - 9\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} difference of two perfect squares. \textbf{Tools:} apply $A^2-B^2=(A-B)(A+B)$ repeatedly. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(x - 7\right) \left(x + 7\right) \left(x^{2} + 49\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} three-term perfect-square pattern. \textbf{Tools:} match $A^2\pm2AB+B^2$. \textbf{Result:} $ 3 \left(9 x^{3} + 1\right)^{2} $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} sum or difference of cubes. \textbf{Tools:} apply the cube identity, after any GCF. \textbf{Result:} $ 2 x \left(x - 1\right) \left(x + 1\right) \left(x^{2} - x + 1\right) \left(x^{2} + x + 1\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} monic quadratic trinomial. \textbf{Tools:} find two integers with sum $-b$ and product $c$. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(x - 5\right) \left(x + 2\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} nonmonic quadratic trinomial. \textbf{Tools:} split the middle term or use an $ac$ method, then group. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(5 x - 7\right) \left(7 x + 8\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} four or more terms with a repeated binomial after grouping. \textbf{Tools:} group terms and extract the common binomial. \textbf{Result:} $ \left(3 x - 2\right) \left(4 x^{2} + 3 x + 1\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\item \textbf{Shape:} quadratic pattern in $x^3$. \textbf{Tools:} substitute $u=x^3$, factor in $u$, then replace $u$. \textbf{Result:} $ 2 x^{2} \left(x^{3} - 5\right) \left(x^{3} - 4\right) $ \textbf{Check:} multiply the proposed factors to recover the original polynomial.
\end{enumerate}
\chapter*{Before Declaring the Algebra Complete}
\addcontentsline{toc}{chapter}{Before Declaring the Algebra Complete}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*]
\item I identified terms and factors correctly.
\item I recorded restrictions from the original expression.
\item I factored before cancelling.
\item I cancelled only entire common factors.
\item I used the least common denominator at its greatest required powers.
\item I inserted zero coefficients in polynomial division when powers were missing.
\item My remainder has degree smaller than the divisor.
\item I made an improper rational expression proper before partial fractions.
\item I factored the denominator completely and wrote every required partial-fraction term.
\item I chose coefficient methods appropriate to the denominator shape.
\item I verified the final result.
\end{itemize}
